Model before rewriting:

letting integerDomain be domain int(-4..3)
find a: integerDomain
find b: int(-3..3)
find p: int(-12..12)
find quotient: int(-4..4)

such that

b != 0,
p = product([a,b;int(1..)]),
quotient = (a / b)

--

b
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b
new variables:
  find b#order_1: bool
  find b#order_2: bool
  find b#order_3: bool
  find b#order_4: bool
  find b#order_5: bool
  find b#order_6: bool
  find b#order_7: bool
new constraints:
  b#order_1
  or([!(b#order_2),b#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(b#order_3),b#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([!(b#order_4),b#order_3;int(1..)])
  or([!(b#order_5),b#order_4;int(1..)])
  or([!(b#order_6),b#order_5;int(1..)])
  or([!(b#order_7),b#order_6;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [b#order_1,b#order_2,b#order_3,b#order_4,b#order_5,b#order_6,b#order_7;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) >= -3,SATInt(Order, [b#order_1,b#order_2,b#order_3,b#order_4,b#order_5,b#order_6,b#order_7;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
